═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: XrayPing.kt
PATH: app/src/main/java/com/lumen/app/ping/XrayPing.kt
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
package com.lumen.app.ping

import android.content.Context
import com.lumen.core.config.builder.XrayConfigBuilder
import com.lumen.core.config.parser.ParsedNode
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.async
import kotlinx.coroutines.awaitAll
import kotlinx.coroutines.coroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Semaphore
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withPermit
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull

/**
 * Measures VLESS nodes on the in-process Xray core exposed by
 * AndroidLibXrayLite (libv2ray.Libv2ray).
 *
 * The previous process-based version spawned one Xray binary per node,
 * which paid a full runtime start plus a SOCKS loopback hop on every
 * probe and is the reason its latency ran 3-5x higher than the number
 * V2rayNG reports for the same server. This version calls
 * Libv2ray.measureOutboundDelay directly: the core runs in the app
 * process, the target URL is dialed from inside it, and the reported
 * millisecond count is the actual round trip through the proxy.
 *
 * The per-node worker is serialized inside AndroidLibXrayLite, so the
 * Semaphore only limits how many coroutines sit in the queue; it is kept
 * to bound memory and to keep behaviour identical to the previous shape.
 */
class XrayPing(private val context: Context) {

    data class PingResult(
        val latencies: Map<Int, Int>,
        val log: String,
    )

    @Suppress("unused")
    // AndroidLibXrayLite's MeasureOutboundDelay uses a process-wide
    // log handler (RegisterHandlerCreator) shared by every instance.
    // Twelve parallel measures close each other's stdout pipe and
    // roughly 30% of healthy nodes fail with "read/write on closed
    // pipe". Four matches V2rayNG's default and drops the failure
    // rate to single digits without slowing the batch: two waves of
    // nine nodes at ~500 ms per node lands in ~1 s.
    private val concurrency = Semaphore(1)

    suspend fun measure(
        nodes: List<ParsedNode>,
        timeoutMs: Int = 2_500,
    ): PingResult = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
            "XRAY_CALLED",
            "measure() entered nodes.size=${nodes.size}"
        )
        if (nodes.isEmpty()) return@withContext PingResult(emptyMap(), "empty node list")

        com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
            "XRAY_START",
            "nodes=${nodes.size} jniAvailable=${XrayBridge.isAvailable()} " +
                "xrayVersion=${XrayBridge.version()} names=${nodes.joinToString(",") { it.name }}"
        )

        if (!XrayBridge.isAvailable()) {
            return@withContext PingResult(
                emptyMap(),
                "XrayBridge unavailable (gomobile AAR missing from this build)"
            )
        }
        XrayBridge.attachFilesDir(context.applicationContext.filesDir.absolutePath)

        val latencies = withTimeoutOrNull(BATCH_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            coroutineScope {
                nodes.indices.map { index ->
                    async(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        concurrency.withPermit {
                            index to pingOne(nodes[index], timeoutMs)
                        }
                    }
                }.awaitAll()
            }
        }?.toMap()?.filterValues { it > 0 } ?: emptyMap()

        val measured = latencies.keys.toList().sorted()
        val missing = nodes.indices.filter { it !in latencies.keys }
        com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
            "XRAY_SUMMARY",
            "measured=${latencies.size}/${nodes.size} ok=$measured fail=$missing"
        )
        PingResult(
            latencies,
            "xray(jni) measured ${latencies.size}/${nodes.size}"
        )
    }

    private suspend fun pingOne(node: ParsedNode, timeoutMs: Int): Int {
        // Per-node trace path. The previous version wrote every probe to
        // one shared "xray-measure.err.log", so a parallel batch mixed
        // four nodes' debug output into the same file and the XRAY_FAIL
        // line for one node carried another node's dial log. The config
        // and the reader now agree on a name that belongs to this node
        // only.
        val tracePath = java.io.File(
            context.applicationContext.filesDir,
            "xray-measure-${node.name.hashCode()}.err.log"
        ).absolutePath

        val configJson = try {
            XrayConfigBuilder.buildMeasureConfig(
                node,
                context.applicationContext.filesDir.absolutePath,
                tracePath
            )
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                "XRAY_FAIL",
                "node=${node.name} reason=config-${e.javaClass.simpleName} msg=${e.message}"
            )
            return -1
        }
        // TEMP: dump the config we hand to the JNI so a parsing or field
        // mismatch is visible in-app without adb.
        com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
            "XRAY_CFG",
            "node=${node.name} cfg=${configJson.replace("\n", " ")}"
        )
        // Wipe the trace file for this node only.
        runCatching {
            java.io.File(tracePath).writeText("")
        }
        // AAR internal budget is 12s x 2 attempts = up to 24s per dead
        // node; a batch of 18 nodes with 6 concurrent workers serializes
        // behind those and the UI settles in 20+ seconds. Bound the
        // coroutine at timeoutMs+500 so a dead node frees its Semaphore
        // slot early; the JNI thread keeps running to completion in the
        // background, but no consumer waits on it.
        val result = withTimeoutOrNull(timeoutMs.toLong() + 500L) {
            XrayBridge.measureOutboundDelay(configJson, TEST_URL)
        } ?: run {
            com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                "XRAY_FAIL",
                "node=${node.name} reason=kotlin-timeout-${timeoutMs + 500}ms"
            )
            -1
        }
        if (result <= 0) {
            // Xray writes its own debug trace to MEASURE_LOG_PATH when the
            // measure config asks for it. Reading the tail of that file is
            // the only way to see the reason a probe failed without a
            // logcat connection.
            val trace = runCatching {
                val f = java.io.File(tracePath)
                if (f.isFile && f.length() > 0L) {
                    val text: String = f.readText()
                    text.takeLast(4000).replace("\n", " | ")
                } else "(no trace)"
            }.getOrDefault("(read failed)")
            com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                "XRAY_FAIL",
                "node=${node.name} result=$result trace=$trace"
            )
        }
        return result
    }

    companion object {
        // HTTP, not HTTPS. TLS to a public target adds 3-4 round trips
        // through the tunnel that the ping pass does not need.
        // Use a raw IP so Xray's internal dialer does not need its DNS app
        // (which AndroidLibXrayLite strips out of the measure config). A
        // domain here costs an extra DNS lookup inside a resolver-less
        // core: the first attempt times out, the second succeeds from
        // cache, and the probe takes ~6s instead of ~2.5s.
        // 1.1.1.1 answers 301 -> https://one.one.one.one/generate_204 -> 404
        private const val TEST_URL = "http://cp.cloudflare.com/generate_204"
        private const val BATCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 45_000L
    }
}


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: XrayBridge.kt
PATH: app/src/main/java/com/lumen/app/ping/XrayBridge.kt
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
package com.lumen.app.ping

import android.util.Log
import libv2ray.Libv2ray

/**
 * Thin wrapper over the gomobile-bound Xray core (AndroidLibXrayLite).
 *
 * Every call runs the Xray core in-process: no binary spawn, no SOCKS
 * loopback, no per-node process teardown. That is the same shape V2rayNG
 * uses and the reason its latency matches Connect on the same server.
 *
 * The class is loaded lazily. If the AAR is missing (a build that did
 * not run the gomobile step) [isAvailable] returns false and
 * [measureOutboundDelay] returns -1, so the caller can log and move on
 * without a crash.
 */
internal object XrayBridge {

    private const val TAG = "XrayBridge"

    private val available: Boolean by lazy {
        try {
            Class.forName("libv2ray.Libv2ray")
            true
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "libv2ray.Libv2ray not present: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}")
            false
        }
    }

    fun isAvailable(): Boolean = available

    /**
     * Round-trip delay in milliseconds through the outbound at
     * outbounds[0] of [configJson], or -1 on any failure.
     */
    fun measureOutboundDelay(
        configJson: String,
        url: String,
    ): Int {
        if (!available) return -1
        initCoreEnvOnce()
        return try {
            val raw = Libv2ray.measureOutboundDelay(configJson, url)
            if (raw <= 0) {
                // gomobile maps (int64, error) to a Java method that throws
                // on a non-nil error; a plain -1 without a throw means the
                // Go side returned (value, nil) or the binding swallowed the
                // error. Log it either way so the in-app Logs screen has it.
                com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                    "XRAY_JNI_RAW",
                    "raw=$raw url=$url"
                )
                -1
            } else raw.toInt()
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                "XRAY_JNI_ERR",
                "${e.javaClass.simpleName}: ${e.message}"
            )
            Log.w(TAG, "measureOutboundDelay: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}: ${e.message}")
            -1
        }
    }

    /**
     * The AAR exposes no version accessor, so report whether the class
     * loaded at all. A real version string can be added once a
     * per-build constant is wired through the AndroidManifest.
     */
    fun version(): String = if (available) "jni" else "unavailable"

    /**
     * Core environment bootstrap. V2rayNG calls this once before any
     * Libv2ray entry point; without it measureOutboundDelay returns -1
     * with no error because the Go side never gets the files directory
     * it uses for the internal cache and geoip data.
     */
    private val initLock = Any()
    @Volatile private var initDone = false

    fun attachFilesDir(path: String) {
        filesDirPath = path
    }

    @Volatile private var filesDirPath: String = ""

    private fun initCoreEnvOnce() {
        if (initDone) return
        synchronized(initLock) {
            if (initDone) return
            try {
                if (filesDirPath.isNotEmpty()) {
                    Libv2ray.initCoreEnv(filesDirPath, "")
                    com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                        "XRAY_JNI_INIT",
                        "initCoreEnv ok filesDir=$filesDirPath"
                    )
                } else {
                    com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                        "XRAY_JNI_INIT",
                        "filesDir path not set; skipping initCoreEnv"
                    )
                }
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                    "XRAY_JNI_INIT_ERR",
                    "${e.javaClass.simpleName}: ${e.message}"
                )
            }
            initDone = true
        }
    }
}


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: BulkSocksPinger.kt
PATH: app/src/main/java/com/lumen/app/ping/BulkSocksPinger.kt
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
package com.lumen.app.ping

import android.content.Context
import com.lumen.core.config.builder.SingboxConfigBuilder
import com.lumen.core.config.builder.SingboxConfigOptions
import com.lumen.core.config.builder.XrayConfigBuilder
import com.lumen.core.config.parser.ParsedNode
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.async
import kotlinx.coroutines.awaitAll
import kotlinx.coroutines.coroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Semaphore
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withPermit
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.util.UUID
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/**
 * One core process carries every node as a SOCKS inbound + proxy outbound
 * pair, so the whole batch measures in one spawn instead of one per node.
 *
 * The per-node shape [XrayPing] / [SingboxSocksPinger] use is correct
 * (same config Connect will load) but pays a process startup per node:
 * 30 nodes is 30 spawns, and on a mid-range phone that is 20+ seconds
 * before the last row settles. V2rayNG avoids this by calling the core
 * in-process; the closest thing available without a JNI binding is one
 * process with N inbounds, which is what this class does.
 *
 * Node order is preserved: index i in the input maps to index i in the
 * result. Missing indices did not answer.
 */
class BulkSocksPinger(private val context: Context) {

    enum class Core { XRAY, SINGBOX }

    data class PingResult(
        val latencies: Map<Int, Int>,
        val log: String,
    )

    /**
     * Each probe is a loopback SOCKS handshake against a local listener, so
     * there is no carrier latency and no per-connection cost beyond the
     * loopback round trip. 32 is well below the file descriptor ceiling
     * Android grants a single process.
     */
    private val probeConcurrency = Semaphore(32)

    suspend fun measure(
        nodes: List<ParsedNode>,
        core: Core,
        options: SingboxConfigOptions = SingboxConfigOptions(),
        timeoutMs: Int = 2_500,
    ): PingResult = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        if (nodes.isEmpty()) return@withContext PingResult(emptyMap(), "empty node list")

        val app = context.applicationContext
        val binaryName = if (core == Core.XRAY) "libxray.so" else "libsingbox.so"
        val binary = File(app.applicationInfo.nativeLibraryDir, binaryName)
        if (!binary.isFile) {
            return@withContext PingResult(emptyMap(), "$binaryName missing for this ABI")
        }

        val basePort = pickBasePort(nodes.size)
            ?: return@withContext PingResult(emptyMap(), "no free port range for ${nodes.size} inbounds")

        val workDir = File(app.cacheDir, "bulk-${core.name.lowercase()}-${UUID.randomUUID()}")
            .apply { mkdirs() }
        val configFile = File(workDir, "config.json")
        var process: Process? = null

        try {
            val configJson = try {
                when (core) {
                    Core.XRAY -> XrayConfigBuilder.buildBulkConfig(nodes, basePort)
                    Core.SINGBOX -> SingboxConfigBuilder.buildBulkSocksConfig(nodes, basePort, options)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                return@withContext PingResult(
                    emptyMap(),
                    "config build failed: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}: ${e.message}"
                )
            }
            configFile.writeText(configJson, Charsets.UTF_8)

            process = ProcessBuilder(binary.absolutePath, "run", "-c", configFile.absolutePath)
                .directory(workDir)
                .redirectErrorStream(true)
                .start()

            val startedProcess = process
            val captured = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf<String>())
            Thread({
                runCatching {
                    startedProcess.inputStream.bufferedReader().use { reader ->
                        var line = reader.readLine()
                        while (line != null) {
                            if (captured.size < 60) captured.add(line)
                            line = reader.readLine()
                        }
                    }
                }
            }, "lumen-bulk-log").apply {
                isDaemon = true
                start()
            }

            // Wait for every inbound to bind. A 100-inbound Xray config
            // takes ~1-2 s to come up on a mid-range phone; 8 s covers a
            // cold CPU without making a failed start wait forever.
            val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 8_000L
            var boundCount = 0
            while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline && startedProcess.isAlive) {
                boundCount = (0 until nodes.size).count { isPortBound(basePort + it) }
                if (boundCount == nodes.size) break
                delay(50)
            }
            if (boundCount != nodes.size) {
                val tail = captured.joinToString(" | ").take(600)
                return@withContext PingResult(
                    emptyMap(),
                    "bulk $core: only $boundCount/${nodes.size} inbounds bound; log=$tail"
                )
            }

            val latencies = coroutineScope {
                nodes.indices.map { index ->
                    async(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        probeConcurrency.withPermit {
                            val port = basePort + index
                            val value = runCatching {
                                SocksHttpPing.measure(port, TEST_URL, timeoutMs, httpGet = false)
                            }.getOrDefault(-1)
                            index to value
                        }
                    }
                }.awaitAll()
            }.filter { it.second > 0 }.toMap()

            PingResult(
                latencies,
                "bulk ${core.name.lowercase()} measured ${latencies.size}/${nodes.size}"
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            PingResult(emptyMap(), "bulk ${core.name.lowercase()} crashed: ${e.javaClass.simpleName}: ${e.message}")
        } finally {
            process?.let {
                runCatching { it.destroy() }
                if (it.isAlive) runCatching { it.waitFor(500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS) }
                if (it.isAlive) runCatching { it.destroyForcibly() }
            }
            runCatching { workDir.deleteRecursively() }
        }
    }

    private fun isPortBound(port: Int): Boolean = !runCatching {
        ServerSocket(port, 1, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1")).use { }
        true
    }.getOrDefault(false)

    /**
     * Finds a contiguous free range outside Android's ephemeral range
     * (32768-60999) so a port the OS hands out mid-run cannot collide
     * with an inbound.
     */
    private fun pickBasePort(size: Int): Int? {
        repeat(16) {
            val base = 10_000 + (Math.random() * (30_000 - size).toDouble()).toInt()
            if ((0 until size).all { isPortBound(base + it) == false }) return base
        }
        return null
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TEST_URL = "http://cp.cloudflare.com/generate_204"
    }
}


═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: SocksHttpPing.kt
PATH: app/src/main/java/com/lumen/app/ping/SocksHttpPing.kt
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
package com.lumen.app.ping

import com.lumen.app.vm.PingBudget
import java.io.InputStream
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.Socket
import java.net.URL
import java.util.Locale
import javax.net.ssl.SSLSocket
import javax.net.ssl.SSLSocketFactory

internal object SocksHttpPing {

    fun measure(
        socksPort: Int,
        rawUrl: String,
        timeoutMs: Int,
        httpGet: Boolean,
    ): Int {
        val url = URL(rawUrl.trim().ifBlank { "https://www.gstatic.com/generate_204" })
        val protocol = url.protocol.lowercase(Locale.US)
        if (protocol != "http" && protocol != "https") return -1
        val targetPort = if (url.port > 0) url.port else if (protocol == "https") 443 else 80
        val hostBytes = url.host.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
        if (hostBytes.isEmpty() || hostBytes.size > 255) return -1
        val startedAt = System.nanoTime()
        var stage = "local SOCKS connection"
        // TEMP INSTRUMENTATION: per-stage timing via VpnLogBus so the run
        // is visible in the in-app Logs screen (no adb on the target setup).
        fun mark(label: String) {
            val elapsedMs = (System.nanoTime() - startedAt) / 1_000_000L
            com.lumen.core.vpn.VpnLogBus.info(
                "PINGSTAGE",
                "port=$socksPort t=+${elapsedMs}ms stage=$label"
            )
        }
        return try {
            Socket().use { socket ->
                socket.soTimeout = PingBudget.setupMs(timeoutMs)
                socket.connect(InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", socksPort), timeoutMs)
                mark("t1 loopback-connected")
                stage = "SOCKS greeting"
                val input = socket.getInputStream()
                val output = socket.getOutputStream()
                output.write(byteArrayOf(0x05, 0x01, 0x00))
                output.flush()
                val greeting = readExactly(input, 2)
                if (greeting[0].toInt() != 0x05 || greeting[1].toInt() != 0x00) {
                    throw java.io.IOException("Invalid greeting")
                }
                mark("t2 socks-greeting-done")
                stage = "outbound connect/DNS"
                output.write(
                    byteArrayOf(0x05, 0x01, 0x00, 0x03, hostBytes.size.toByte()) +
                        hostBytes +
                        byteArrayOf((targetPort ushr 8).toByte(), targetPort.toByte())
                )
                output.flush()
                val reply = readExactly(input, 4)
                if (reply[1].toInt() != 0x00) throw java.io.IOException("SOCKS rejected")
                mark("t3 remote-dial-handshake-done")
                when (reply[3].toInt() and 0xFF) {
                    0x01 -> readExactly(input, 4)
                    0x03 -> readExactly(input, readExactly(input, 1)[0].toInt() and 0xFF)
                    0x04 -> readExactly(input, 16)
                    else -> throw java.io.IOException("Unsupported SOCKS address type")
                }
                readExactly(input, 2)

                stage = "TLS handshake"
                val requestSocket: Socket = if (protocol == "https") {
                    val sslSocket = (SSLSocketFactory.getDefault() as SSLSocketFactory)
                        .createSocket(socket, url.host, targetPort, true) as SSLSocket
                    sslSocket.soTimeout = PingBudget.setupMs(timeoutMs)
                    sslSocket.sslParameters = sslSocket.sslParameters.apply {
                        endpointIdentificationAlgorithm = "HTTPS"
                    }
                    sslSocket.startHandshake()
                    sslSocket
                } else {
                    socket
                }
                mark("t4 local-tls-handshake-done")
                val requestStartedAt = System.nanoTime()
                stage = "HTTP response"
                requestSocket.use { active ->
                    active.soTimeout = timeoutMs
                    val activeOut = active.getOutputStream()
                    val path = url.file.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "/"
                    activeOut.write(
                        (
                            "GET $path HTTP/1.1\r\n" +
                                "Host: ${url.host}\r\n" +
                                "User-Agent: Lumen-Ping/Android\r\n" +
                                (if (httpGet) "" else "Range: bytes=0-0\r\n") +
                                "Connection: close\r\n\r\n"
                            ).toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII)
                    )
                    activeOut.flush()
                    if (httpGet) {
                        val status = readStatusLine(active.getInputStream())
                        if (!isSuccessfulHttpPingStatusLine(status)) {
                            throw java.io.IOException("Invalid HTTP response")
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (active.getInputStream().read() < 0) {
                            throw java.io.IOException("Probe stage: $stage (EOF, zero bytes)")
                        }
                    }
                }
                mark("t5 http-response-done")
                val measuredFrom = if (httpGet) requestStartedAt else startedAt
                ((System.nanoTime() - measuredFrom) / 1_000_000L).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            throw java.io.IOException("Probe stage: $stage (${e.javaClass.simpleName})", e)
        }
    }

    private fun readStatusLine(input: InputStream): String? {
        val line = StringBuilder()
        while (line.length < 256) {
            val byte = input.read()
            if (byte < 0) break
            if (byte == '\n'.code) break
            if (byte != '\r'.code) line.append(Char(byte))
        }
        return line.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toString()
    }

    private fun readExactly(input: InputStream, count: Int): ByteArray {
        val result = ByteArray(count)
        var offset = 0
        while (offset < count) {
            val read = input.read(result, offset, count - offset)
            if (read < 0) throw java.io.EOFException("SOCKS proxy closed the connection")
            offset += read
        }
        return result
    }
}

internal fun isSuccessfulHttpPingStatusLine(status: String?): Boolean {
    val value = status?.trim().orEmpty()
    val firstSpace = value.indexOf(' ')
    if (firstSpace <= 0 || !value.substring(0, firstSpace).startsWith("HTTP/", true)) return false
    val code = value.substring(firstSpace + 1)
        .trimStart()
        .takeWhile(Char::isDigit)
        .takeIf { it.length == 3 }
        ?.toIntOrNull()
        ?: return false
    return code in 100..599
}





═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: XrayConfigBuilder.kt
PATH: core/config/src/main/java/com/lumen/core/config/builder/XrayConfigBuilder.kt
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
package com.lumen.core.config.builder

import com.lumen.core.config.parser.ParsedNode
import org.json.JSONObject

/**
 * Translates a [ParsedNode] into an Xray-core JSON config.
 *
 * Mirrors what [SingboxConfigBuilder] does for sing-box, but produces
 * Xray's schema. The two differ in field names and in a few structural
 * choices: "type" on the inbound becomes "protocol", the WS host header
 * lives at wsSettings.host instead of wsSettings.headers.Host, and the
 * TLS insecure flag no longer exists (it was removed in Xray v26 in
 * favour of pinnedPeerCertSha256).
 *
 * Scope is deliberately narrow. The Xray binary is only reached for the
 * two shapes where sing-box-extended's implementation is either slow
 * (xhttp) or refuses to build the config (xhttp+reality). Every other
 * scheme in a subscription keeps going through sing-box. A node whose
 * scheme is not handled here throws, so a caller cannot accidentally
 * route traffic through a half-built config.
 */
object XrayConfigBuilder {

    /**
     * Produces a complete Xray config with a loopback SOCKS inbound on
     * [socksPort] and a single proxy outbound built from [node].
     */
    fun buildConfig(node: ParsedNode, socksPort: Int): String {
        val root = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()

        root["log"] = mapOf("loglevel" to "warning")

        // External resolvers only. The system resolver on an Iranian
        // carrier answers foreign hostnames with the block-page IP; Xray
        // has no pinning path, so the only safe default is to bypass the
        // local resolver entirely for anything resolved through this
        // config.
        root["dns"] = mapOf(
            "servers" to listOf("1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8")
        )

        root["inbounds"] = listOf(
            mapOf(
                "listen" to "127.0.0.1",
                "port" to socksPort,
                "protocol" to "socks",
                "tag" to "socks-in",
                "settings" to mapOf(
                    "auth" to "noauth",
                    "udp" to true
                ),
                // tun2socks forwards DNS through this same SOCKS inbound
                // as plain UDP:53 traffic aimed at a virtual address. Without
                // sniffing, Xray cannot tell a DNS query from any other
                // packet and hands it to the proxy outbound, where the
                // virtual address means nothing to the server and every
                // lookup silently times out. Sniffing lets the routing rule
                // below pull DNS out of the tunnel and answer it locally.
                "sniffing" to mapOf(
                    "enabled" to true,
                    "destOverride" to listOf("http", "tls", "fakedns")
                )
            )
        )

        // The proxy outbound is deliberately first: the delay queries the
        // Clash-style API issues for ping, and any future caller that
        // reaches in by index instead of tag, has to land on the node
        // under test, not on the two utility outbounds below.
        root["outbounds"] = listOf(
            buildOutbound(node),
            // Xray's own resolver dials these freedom/dns outbounds; they
            // are never reached by traffic from socks-in except through
            // the port-53 rule below.
            mapOf(
                "protocol" to "freedom",
                "tag" to "direct"
            ),
            mapOf(
                "protocol" to "dns",
                "tag" to "dns-out"
            )
        )

        // DNS hijack. Every packet with destination port 53 or 853 is
        // answered by the dns outbound using the servers listed above,
        // instead of being tunnelled to the proxy where the virtual
        // destination (172.19.0.2, Lumen's internal resolver) means nothing.
        //
        // Three details, each one something that stopped the rule from
        // matching:
        //   - port must be a string in Xray v26. An int passes config check
        //     and then silently never matches.
        //   - network "udp" is stated explicitly: tun2socks forwards DNS as
        //     UDP through the SOCKS inbound, and without this the rule was
        //     still evaluated but the packet fell to the default outbound.
        //   - inboundTag is deliberately absent. When it was present the
        //     rule never fired for SOCKS UDP-associate traffic, which is the
        //     exact form tun2socks uses.
        root["routing"] = mapOf(
            "domainStrategy" to "AsIs",
            "rules" to listOf(
                mapOf(
                    "type" to "field",
                    "network" to "udp",
                    "port" to "53,853",
                    "outboundTag" to "dns-out"
                ),
                mapOf(
                    "type" to "field",
                    "inboundTag" to listOf("socks-in"),
                    "outboundTag" to "proxy"
                )
            )
        )

        return JSONObject(root).toString(2)
    }

    private fun buildOutbound(node: ParsedNode, tag: String = "proxy"): Map<String, Any?> {
        return when (node.scheme.lowercase()) {
            "vless" -> buildVless(node, tag)
            "vmess" -> buildVmess(node, tag)
            "trojan" -> buildTrojan(node, tag)
            "ss", "shadowsocks" -> buildShadowsocks(node, tag)
            else -> throw IllegalArgumentException(
                "XrayConfigBuilder does not handle scheme '${node.scheme}' yet"
            )
        }
    }

    /**
     * Minimal config for a single outbound delay measurement through the
     * in-process Xray core (AndroidLibXrayLite.measureOutboundDelay).
     *
     * Only outbounds[0] matters to the measurement path; the SOCKS inbound
     * is a placeholder so the config parses. No DNS hijack, no routing
     * rules, no direct/dns-out outbounds.
     */
    fun buildMeasureConfig(
        node: ParsedNode,
        filesDir: String,
        errorLogPath: String = "$filesDir/xray-measure.err.log",
    ): String {
        val root = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        // The error sink must live inside *this* app's files dir. The old
        // constant hardcoded /data/data/com.lumen.app, but the package is
        // net.kramb.lumen, so Xray's logger init failed with ENOENT and
        // aborted the whole instance - which is exactly what the earlier
        // "instance creation failed" XRAY_JNI_ERR line reported.
        root["log"] = mapOf(
            "loglevel" to "debug",
            "error" to errorLogPath
        )

        // VLESS with no TLS/Reality is rejected by Xray 26.x when the
        // server address is a public IP:
        //   "vless without TLS or other encryption is prohibited unless
        //    the server address is a private IP or domain"
        // Providers hand out these nodes with an IP, so we present the
        // outbound with a synthetic domain and let Xray resolve it back
        // to the real IP through the dns.hosts table below. A private IP
        // or an address that already looks like a domain skips the
        // workaround.
        val dnsMap = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
            "servers" to listOf("1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8")
        )

        val outbound = buildOutbound(node, "proxy")
        val needsFakeDomain = node.scheme.equals("vless", ignoreCase = true) &&
            !hasTlsOrReality(outbound) &&
            isPublicIpv4(node.server)
        if (needsFakeDomain) {
            val fakeDomain = node.server.replace('.', '-') + ".sslip.io"
            dnsMap["hosts"] = mapOf(fakeDomain to node.server)
            root["dns"] = dnsMap
            root["outbounds"] = listOf(rewriteAddress(outbound, fakeDomain))
        } else {
            root["dns"] = dnsMap
            root["outbounds"] = listOf(outbound)
        }
        return JSONObject(root).toString(2)
    }

    private fun hasTlsOrReality(outbound: Map<String, Any?>): Boolean {
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val stream = outbound["streamSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: return false
        val sec = stream["security"]?.toString()?.lowercase()
        return sec == "tls" || sec == "reality"
    }

    private fun isPublicIpv4(address: String): Boolean {
        val parts = address.split(".")
        if (parts.size != 4) return false
        val nums = parts.mapNotNull { it.toIntOrNull() }
        if (nums.size != 4 || nums.any { it !in 0..255 }) return false
        val a = nums[0]
        return !(a == 10 || a == 127 || (a == 172 && nums[1] in 16..31) ||
                 (a == 192 && nums[1] == 168) || a == 0 || a >= 224)
    }

    private fun rewriteAddress(outbound: Map<String, Any?>, newAddress: String): Map<String, Any?> {
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val settings = outbound["settings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: return outbound
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val vnext = (settings["vnext"] as? List<*>)?.firstOrNull() as? Map<*, *> ?: return outbound
        val newVnext = vnext.entries.associate { it.key.toString() to it.value }.toMutableMap()
        newVnext["address"] = newAddress
        val newSettings = settings.toMutableMap().apply { put("vnext", listOf(newVnext)) }
        return outbound.toMutableMap().apply { put("settings", newSettings) }
    }

    /**
     * Bulk config: N SOCKS inbounds (one per node) + N proxy outbounds +
     * N routing rules, plus the DNS hijack. Only one process is spawned
     * for the whole batch, which is what makes the bulk ping fast enough
     * to match what the user gets from V2rayNG.
     */
    fun buildBulkConfig(nodes: List<ParsedNode>, basePort: Int): String {
        val root = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        root["log"] = mapOf("loglevel" to "warning")
        root["dns"] = mapOf("servers" to listOf("1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8"))

        val inbounds = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()
        val outbounds = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()
        val rules = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()

        // DNS hijack first so it applies to every inbound.
        rules.add(
            mapOf(
                "type" to "field",
                "network" to "udp",
                "port" to "53,853",
                "outboundTag" to "dns-out"
            )
        )

        nodes.forEachIndexed { index, node ->
            val inTag = "socks-$index"
            val outTag = "proxy-$index"
            inbounds.add(
                mapOf(
                    "listen" to "127.0.0.1",
                    "port" to (basePort + index),
                    "protocol" to "socks",
                    "tag" to inTag,
                    "settings" to mapOf("auth" to "noauth", "udp" to true),
                    "sniffing" to mapOf(
                        "enabled" to true,
                        "destOverride" to listOf("http", "tls", "fakedns")
                    )
                )
            )
            outbounds.add(buildOutbound(node, outTag))
            rules.add(
                mapOf(
                    "type" to "field",
                    "inboundTag" to listOf(inTag),
                    "outboundTag" to outTag
                )
            )
        }

        outbounds.add(mapOf("protocol" to "freedom", "tag" to "direct"))
        outbounds.add(mapOf("protocol" to "dns", "tag" to "dns-out"))

        root["inbounds"] = inbounds
        root["outbounds"] = outbounds
        root["routing"] = mapOf("domainStrategy" to "AsIs", "rules" to rules)

        return JSONObject(root).toString(2)
    }

    private fun buildVless(node: ParsedNode, tag: String = "proxy"): Map<String, Any?> {
        val outbound = node.outbound
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val settings = outbound["settings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
        val vnext = (settings["vnext"] as? List<*>)?.firstOrNull() as? Map<*, *>
        val users = (vnext?.get("users") as? List<*>)?.firstOrNull() as? Map<*, *>

        val address = vnext?.get("address")?.toString() ?: node.server
        val port = (vnext?.get("port") as? Number)?.toInt() ?: node.port
        val uuid = users?.get("id")?.toString() ?: ""
        // Xray requires the field to be present and equal to "none" for
        // plain VLESS. Leaving it out aborts the outbound at load time.
        val encryption = users?.get("encryption")?.toString()
            ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?: "none"
        val flow = users?.get("flow")?.toString().orEmpty()

        val userJson = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
            "id" to uuid,
            "encryption" to encryption
        )
        // flow only exists for VLESS+TCP+TLS/REALITY; the parser already
        // drops it for other transports, so no extra gate is needed here.
        // Reality on plain tcp requires xtls-rprx-vision; providers that
        // ship the share link without flow= rely on the client to add it,
        // which V2rayNG does and which we now match.
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val streamForFlow = outbound["streamSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?>
        val netForFlow = streamForFlow?.get("network")?.toString()?.lowercase()
        val secForFlow = streamForFlow?.get("security")?.toString()?.lowercase()
        // An http-obfuscated tcp transport is mutually exclusive with
        // vision flow: Xray refuses the pair with "XTLS only supports TLS
        // and REALITY directly for now". When LinkParser produced a
        // tcpSettings.header.type=http block, do not force vision.
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val tcpForFlow = streamForFlow?.get("tcpSettings") as? Map<String, Any?>
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val tcpHeaderForFlow = tcpForFlow?.get("header") as? Map<String, Any?>
        val tcpHeaderType = tcpHeaderForFlow?.get("type")?.toString()?.lowercase()
        val effectiveFlow = when {
            flow.isNotEmpty() -> flow
            tcpHeaderType == "http" -> ""
            secForFlow == "reality" && (netForFlow == null || netForFlow == "tcp") ->
                "xtls-rprx-vision"
            else -> ""
        }
        if (effectiveFlow.isNotEmpty()) userJson["flow"] = effectiveFlow

        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val streamSettings = outbound["streamSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()

        return mapOf(
            "protocol" to "vless",
            "tag" to tag,
            "settings" to mapOf(
                "vnext" to listOf(
                    mapOf(
                        "address" to address,
                        "port" to port,
                        "users" to listOf(userJson)
                    )
                )
            ),
            "streamSettings" to buildStreamSettings(streamSettings)
        )
    }

    /**
     * VMess outbound. The parser writes the same vnext/users shape as
     * VLESS; only the user fields differ (alterId + security instead of
     * encryption + flow). Xray requires `alterId` to be present as a
     * number and defaults `security` to "auto".
     */
    private fun buildVmess(node: ParsedNode, tag: String = "proxy"): Map<String, Any?> {
        val outbound = node.outbound
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val settings = outbound["settings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
        val vnext = (settings["vnext"] as? List<*>)?.firstOrNull() as? Map<*, *>
        val users = (vnext?.get("users") as? List<*>)?.firstOrNull() as? Map<*, *>

        val address = vnext?.get("address")?.toString() ?: node.server
        val port = (vnext?.get("port") as? Number)?.toInt() ?: node.port
        val uuid = users?.get("id")?.toString() ?: ""
        val alterId = (users?.get("alterId") as? Number)?.toInt() ?: 0
        val security = users?.get("security")?.toString()
            ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?: "auto"

        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val streamSettings = outbound["streamSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()

        return mapOf(
            "protocol" to "vmess",
            "tag" to tag,
            "settings" to mapOf(
                "vnext" to listOf(
                    mapOf(
                        "address" to address,
                        "port" to port,
                        "users" to listOf(
                            mapOf(
                                "id" to uuid,
                                "alterId" to alterId,
                                "security" to security
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )
            ),
            "streamSettings" to buildStreamSettings(streamSettings)
        )
    }

    /**
     * Trojan outbound. The parser stores a single server map with the
     * password inline; Xray expects the same shape under settings.servers
     * with the field name `password`.
     */
    private fun buildTrojan(node: ParsedNode, tag: String = "proxy"): Map<String, Any?> {
        val outbound = node.outbound
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val settings = outbound["settings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
        val server = (settings["servers"] as? List<*>)?.firstOrNull() as? Map<*, *>

        val address = server?.get("address")?.toString() ?: node.server
        val port = (server?.get("port") as? Number)?.toInt() ?: node.port
        val password = server?.get("password")?.toString() ?: ""

        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val streamSettings = outbound["streamSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()

        return mapOf(
            "protocol" to "trojan",
            "tag" to tag,
            "settings" to mapOf(
                "servers" to listOf(
                    mapOf(
                        "address" to address,
                        "port" to port,
                        "password" to password
                    )
                )
            ),
            "streamSettings" to buildStreamSettings(streamSettings)
        )
    }

    /**
     * Shadowsocks outbound. Same server shape as Trojan but with method
     * and an optional SIP003 plugin. Xray's SS outbound carries no
     * streamSettings; ws/grpc/... are not applicable to this protocol.
     */
    private fun buildShadowsocks(node: ParsedNode, tag: String = "proxy"): Map<String, Any?> {
        val outbound = node.outbound
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val settings = outbound["settings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
        val server = (settings["servers"] as? List<*>)?.firstOrNull() as? Map<*, *>

        val address = server?.get("address")?.toString() ?: node.server
        val port = (server?.get("port") as? Number)?.toInt() ?: node.port
        val method = server?.get("method")?.toString() ?: ""
        val password = server?.get("password")?.toString() ?: ""

        val serverJson = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
            "address" to address,
            "port" to port,
            "method" to method,
            "password" to password
        )
        server?.get("plugin")?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { serverJson["plugin"] = it }
        server?.get("plugin_opts")?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { serverJson["plugin_opts"] = it }

        return mapOf(
            "protocol" to "shadowsocks",
            "tag" to tag,
            "settings" to mapOf("servers" to listOf(serverJson))
        )
    }

    private fun buildStreamSettings(stream: Map<String, Any?>): Map<String, Any?> {
        val network = stream["network"]?.toString()?.lowercase() ?: "tcp"
        val security = stream["security"]?.toString()?.lowercase() ?: ""

        val result = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        result["network"] = network
        if (security.isNotEmpty() && security != "none") {
            result["security"] = security
        }

        when (security) {
            "tls" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val tls = stream["tlsSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
                result["tlsSettings"] = buildTlsSettings(tls)
            }
            "reality" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val reality = stream["realitySettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
                result["realitySettings"] = buildRealitySettings(reality)
            }
        }

        when (network) {
            "ws" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val ws = stream["wsSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
                result["wsSettings"] = buildWsSettings(ws)
            }
            "xhttp" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val xhttp = stream["xhttpSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
                result["xhttpSettings"] = buildXhttpSettings(xhttp)
            }
            "grpc" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val grpc = stream["grpcSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
                result["grpcSettings"] = buildGrpcSettings(grpc)
            }
            "http", "h2" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val http = stream["httpSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
                result["httpSettings"] = buildHttpSettings(http)
            }
            "httpupgrade" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val hu = stream["httpupgradeSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
                result["httpupgradeSettings"] = buildHttpUpgradeSettings(hu)
            }
            "tcp" -> {
                // VLESS over TCP with an HTTP disguise. LinkParser already
                // builds the exact Xray-native shape, so the only work here
                // is passing it through when present.
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val tcp = stream["tcpSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: emptyMap()
                if (tcp.isNotEmpty()) {
                    result["tcpSettings"] = tcp
                }
            }
        }

        return result
    }

    /**
     * Maps the share link's fp= value onto an Xray-accepted fingerprint.
     *
     * "unsafe" is what many Iranian subscription providers ship: it tells
     * the client to use a plain Go TLS handshake with no ClientHello
     * disguise. Cloudflare's edge detects that handshake and resets the
     * connection within ~20 ms for the SNIs these nodes use, which is
     * exactly the fast-fail our ping logs show. V2rayNG silently promotes
     * "unsafe" to "chrome"; this does the same. An empty fp= also lands
     * on "chrome" rather than letting Xray fall back to the bare Go
     * handshake.
     */
    private fun normalizeFingerprint(raw: String?): String {
        val v = raw?.trim()?.lowercase().orEmpty()
        if (v.isEmpty() || v == "unsafe") return "chrome"
        // "random" and "randomized" are legal in Xray but pick a fresh
        // profile per connection, so the ClientHello shape changes on
        // every dial and a node that lands a ping on one attempt can hit
        // a reset on the next. V2rayNG pins them; so do we.
        if (v == "random" || v == "randomized") return "chrome"
        // Xray aborts the whole config at load time when the fp value is
        // not in its uTLS registry (utls.UTLSIdToSpec returns an error),
        // so a single typo in a share link kills an otherwise healthy
        // node. Fall back to chrome for anything outside the accepted set.
        return if (v in XRAY_FINGERPRINTS) v else "chrome"
    }

    // Values Xray 26.9.9 accepts from refraction-networking/utls.
    private val XRAY_FINGERPRINTS = setOf(
        "chrome", "firefox", "safari", "ios",
        "android", "edge", "360", "qq"
    )

    private fun buildTlsSettings(tls: Map<String, Any?>): Map<String, Any?> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        tls["serverName"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { result["serverName"] = it }
        (tls["alpn"] as? List<*>)?.let { alpn ->
            val cleaned = alpn.mapNotNull { it?.toString() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
            if (cleaned.isNotEmpty()) result["alpn"] = cleaned
        }
        // The uTLS fingerprint selects the ClientHello shape. Without it,
        // Xray falls back to the plain Go handshake, which Cloudflare's
        // edge resets for the browser-only SNIs our test set uses.
        result["fingerprint"] = normalizeFingerprint(tls["fingerprint"]?.toString())
        return result
    }

    private fun buildRealitySettings(reality: Map<String, Any?>): Map<String, Any?> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        reality["serverName"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { result["serverName"] = it }
        result["fingerprint"] = normalizeFingerprint(reality["fingerprint"]?.toString())
        reality["publicKey"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { result["publicKey"] = it }
        reality["shortId"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { result["shortId"] = it }
        reality["spiderX"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { result["spiderX"] = it }
        return result
    }

    private fun buildWsSettings(ws: Map<String, Any?>): Map<String, Any?> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        ws["path"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { result["path"] = it }
        // The parser stores the host under wsSettings.headers.Host, which
        // is how sing-box reads it. Xray v26 promotes it to a top-level
        // wsSettings.host field and warns when the header form is used.
        // Lift it here so the same ParsedNode feeds both builders without
        // a second parser path.
        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
        val headers = ws["headers"] as? Map<String, Any?>
        headers?.get("Host")?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { result["host"] = it }
        return result
    }

    private fun buildXhttpSettings(xhttp: Map<String, Any?>): Map<String, Any?> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        xhttp["path"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { result["path"] = it }
        xhttp["host"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { result["host"] = it }
        // mode drives the transport shape. "auto" is what sing-box
        // silently downgrades to packet-up; Xray honours it, but
        // stream-one is what the test server expects and what produced
        // 271 ms outside Lumen. Default to stream-one when the share
        // link omits the field.
        val mode = xhttp["mode"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?: "stream-one"
        result["mode"] = mode
        return result
    }

    private fun buildGrpcSettings(grpc: Map<String, Any?>): Map<String, Any?> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        grpc["serviceName"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { result["serviceName"] = it }
        grpc["authority"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { result["authority"] = it }
        // multiMode is what V2rayNG enables for shared gRPC tunnels. Xray
        // v26 needs the field present; omit it and the transport runs in
        // singleMode, which is what the bundled sing-box defaults to.
        grpc["multiMode"]?.let { result["multiMode"] = it }
        return result
    }

    private fun buildHttpSettings(http: Map<String, Any?>): Map<String, Any?> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        (http["host"] as? List<*>)?.let { hosts ->
            val cleaned = hosts.mapNotNull { it?.toString() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
            if (cleaned.isNotEmpty()) result["host"] = cleaned
        }
        http["path"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { result["path"] = it }
        return result
    }

    private fun buildHttpUpgradeSettings(hu: Map<String, Any?>): Map<String, Any?> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
        hu["path"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { result["path"] = it }
        hu["host"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { result["host"] = it }
        return result
    }
}

PATH: core/config/src/main/java/com/lumen/core/config/builder/XrayRouter.kt
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
package com.lumen.core.config.builder

import com.lumen.core.config.parser.ParsedNode

/**
 * Decides which core a node should run on.
 *
 * Xray handles the two shapes sing-box-extended mishandles:
 * - xhttp: sing-box's scheduler silently downgrades mode=auto to
 *   packet-up, the slowest transport shape, and the measured RTT runs
 *   3-4x the real number.
 * - reality over TCP: the extended core rejects the outbound at
 *   config decode.
 *
 * Every other scheme stays on sing-box, where the existing pinning
 * and multiplexing already work. The predicate is written against
 * ParsedNode.outbound (the same map SingboxConfigBuilder reads) so
 * both builders agree on what a node is.
 */
object XrayRouter {

    /**
     * Xray-native VLESS transports. Reality over any of these and every
     * XHTTP share goes through Xray; the rest of VLESS (and every other
     * scheme) keeps its current builder. The allow-list must stay in sync
     * with the networks XrayConfigBuilder knows how to translate - a
     * network added here before the builder learns it aborts the whole
     * config at load time and the AUTO pool with it.
     */
    private val XRAY_VLESS_NETWORKS = setOf("tcp", "ws", "xhttp", "grpc", "http", "h2", "httpupgrade")

    fun shouldUseXray(node: ParsedNode): Boolean {
        return when (node.scheme.lowercase()) {
            // VLESS, VMess, and Trojan all carry streamSettings in the same
            // shape and all three share the same network gate. A node whose
            // transport the builder cannot translate (kcp, quic) stays on
            // sing-box, where the transport still works.
            "vless", "vmess", "trojan" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val stream = node.outbound["streamSettings"] as? Map<String, Any?> ?: return false
                val network = stream["network"]?.toString()?.lowercase() ?: "tcp"
                if (network !in XRAY_VLESS_NETWORKS) return false
                // Xray 26.9.x rejects VLESS / VMess / Trojan without TLS or
                // REALITY at config load time when the server address is a
                // public IP. No config knob bypasses it. Those nodes go to
                // sing-box; it has no such restriction and dials them fine.
                val security = stream["security"]?.toString()?.lowercase().orEmpty()
                security == "tls" || security == "reality"
            }
            // Xray 26.9.x removed the none/plain Shadowsocks ciphers. A
            // node that carries one cannot be loaded by Xray; send it to
            // sing-box. Every other cipher stays on Xray.
            "ss", "shadowsocks" -> {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val settings = node.outbound["settings"] as? Map<String, Any?>
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val servers = settings?.get("servers") as? List<*>
                val first = servers?.firstOrNull() as? Map<*, *>
                val method = first?.get("method")?.toString()?.lowercase().orEmpty()
                method != "none" && method != "plain"
            }
            else -> false
        }
    }
}

FILE: SingboxConfigBuilder.kt

package com.lumen.core.config.builder

import com.lumen.core.config.normalizer.AmneziaWGNormalizer
import com.lumen.core.config.normalizer.OpenVpnConfigNormalizer
import com.lumen.core.config.parser.LinkParser
import com.lumen.core.config.parser.ParsedNode
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.util.Base64

data class SingboxConfigOptions(
    val tunMode: Boolean = true,
    val tunAddressIPv4: String = "172.19.0.1/30",
    val tunAddressIPv6: String = "fdfe:dcba:9876::1/126",
    // 1500 is what every path between the phone and the server can carry; the old
    // 9000 default only worked for protocols that fragment for themselves and made
    // OpenVPN drop full-size packets.
    val tunMtu: Int = 1500,
    val tunStrictRoute: Boolean = true,
    val tunStack: String = "mixed",
    val localSocksPort: Int = 10808,
    val localHttpPort: Int = 10809,
    /** Per-runtime loopback port used by the in-app OpenVPN obfs relay. */
    val obfsLocalPort: Int = 10871,
    val allowLanConnections: Boolean = false,
    val inboundUdpTimeoutSeconds: Int = 0,
    val multiplexEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val multiplexConcurrency: Int = 8,
    val multiplexProtocol: String = "smux",
    val multiplexMinStreams: Int = 4,
    val multiplexPadding: Boolean = true,
    val multiplexBrutalEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val multiplexBrutalUpMbps: Int = 0,
    val multiplexBrutalDownMbps: Int = 0,
    val outboundTcpFastOpen: Boolean = false,
    val outboundTcpMultiPath: Boolean = false,
    val outboundUdpFragment: Boolean = false,
    val outboundConnectTimeoutSeconds: Int = 0,
    val udpOverTcp: Boolean = false,
    val enableFinalFragment: Boolean = false,
    val tlsFragmentFallbackDelayMs: Int = 500,
    val fragmentPackets: String = "tlshello",
    val fragmentLength: String = "50-100",
    val fragmentDelay: String = "10-20",
    val preferIpv6: Boolean = false,
    val blockQuic: Boolean = false,
    val sniffRouteOnly: Boolean = false,
    val proxyDnsServer: String = "cloudflare-dns.com",
    val directDnsServer: String = "1.1.1.1",
    val dnsMode: String = "automatic",
    /** Exact sing-box-extended DNS section used only when [dnsMode] is `json`. */
    val dnsCustomJson: String = "",
    val dnsDirectServers: List<String> = listOf("1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8"),
    /**
     * Resolvers of the physical network, as the OS reports them. Used only for
     * `dns-system`, the literal bootstrap resolver every other DNS server is
     * resolved through. Empty keeps the hard-coded public fallback.
     */
    val systemDnsServers: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val dnsProxyServers: List<String> = listOf("cloudflare-dns.com", "dns.google"),
    val dnsDirectType: String = "udp",
    val dnsProxyType: String = "https",
    val dnsDirectStrategy: String = "ipv4_only",
    val dnsProxyStrategy: String = "ipv4_only",
    val dnsHijackEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val dnsFakeIpEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val dnsFakeIpRangeIPv4: String = "198.18.0.0/15",
    val dnsFakeIpRangeIPv6: String = "fc00::/18",
    val dnsParallelQuery: Boolean = false,
    val dnsOptimisticCache: Boolean = false,
    val dnsIndependentCache: Boolean = true,
    val dnsDisableCache: Boolean = false,
    val dnsCacheCapacity: Int = 0,
    val dnsClientSubnet: String = "",
    val dnsDirectRuleStrategy: String = "",
    val dnsGeoCheck: Boolean = true,
    val dnsProxyIpv4Only: Boolean = true,
    val dnsHosts: Map<String, List<String>> = emptyMap(),
    /**
     * Server hostname -> addresses the app resolved before the tunnel existed,
     * for outbounds whose hostname the local network answers with a sinkhole.
     * A hijacking carrier (Iran's 10.10.34.x block page) does not fail a query,
     * it answers it, so no fallback chain inside the core can ever step past a
     * poisoned reply - the core has no way to judge an answer bogus. The app
     * can: it compares resolvers and drops private/reserved addresses, then
     * pins what is left here. Only the matching outbounds are pointed at this
     * table; everything else keeps the normal bootstrap chain.
     */
    val pinnedServerIps: Map<String, List<String>> = emptyMap(),
    val dnsOverrideEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val dnsOverrideHostname: String = "",
    val dnsOverrideIpv4: String = "",
    val domainResolverStrategy: String = "",
    val sniffTimeoutMs: Int = 0,
    val sniffers: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val logLevel: String = "info",
    /**
     * Loopback port of the sing-box Clash API. Zero disables it. When set,
     * the config exposes `experimental.clash_api` at 127.0.0.1:<port>, and
     * the app can read per-outbound latency from `/proxies` instead of
     * spawning one sing-box per node. Only used by the bulk ping path.
     */
    val clashApiPort: Int = 0,
    val urlTestUrl: String = "https://www.gstatic.com/generate_204",
    val urlTestIntervalMinutes: Int = 3,
    val urlTestToleranceMs: Int = 50,
    val urlTestIdleTimeoutMinutes: Int = 0,
    val urlTestInterruptExistConnections: Boolean = true,
    val bypassLan: Boolean = false,
    val cacheFileEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val cacheFilePath: String = "",
    val ntpEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val ntpServer: String = "time.apple.com",
    /**
     * Provider selected on the Geo resources screen.  The UI historically downloaded
     * legacy .dat files while this builder silently used SagerNet .srs URLs, so choosing
     * the Iranian/Russian source had no effect on the configuration at all.
     */
    val geoResourceSource: String =
        "https://github.com/runetfreedom/russia-v2ray-rules-dat/",
    val directDomains: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val directIpCidrs: List<String> = emptyList(),
    /**
     * Socks5 authorization for the local inbound. While it is on, the local
     * SOCKS proxy only accepts these credentials and the plain HTTP inbound is
     * not published at all, so a LAN neighbour cannot use the tunnel.
     */
    val socksAuthEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val socksUsername: String = "",
    val socksPassword: String = "",
    /**
     * Directory holding rule sets the app already downloaded (`<tag>.srs`).
     * A tag found here is emitted as a `local` rule set, which removes the
     * remote fetch the core otherwise performs while it starts.
     */
    val geoRuleSetDir: String = "",
    /**
     * Drop a geo rule whose rule set is not in [geoRuleSetDir] instead of
     * emitting a `remote` one. A remote rule set the core cannot download is
     * fatal: it aborts startup, which left users with geo rules unable to
     * connect at all. The app sets this and downloads the sets itself.
     */
    val requireLocalRuleSets: Boolean = false
)

object SingboxConfigBuilder {

    // Exact outbound methods accepted by the bundled sing-box-extended core.
    // Keep this scoped to Shadowsocks: VMess and Sudoku intentionally use the
    // otherwise similar `chacha20-poly1305` spelling.
    private val SHADOWSOCKS_METHODS = setOf(
        "none",
        "aes-128-gcm",
        "aes-192-gcm",
        "aes-256-gcm",
        "chacha20-ietf-poly1305",
        "xchacha20-ietf-poly1305",
        "2022-blake3-aes-128-gcm",
        "2022-blake3-aes-256-gcm",
        "2022-blake3-chacha20-poly1305",
        "aes-128-ctr",
        "aes-192-ctr",
        "aes-256-ctr",
        "aes-128-cfb",
        "aes-192-cfb",
        "aes-256-cfb",
        "rc4-md5",
        "chacha20-ietf",
        "xchacha20"
    )
    private val SHADOWSOCKS_PLUGINS = setOf("obfs-local", "v2ray-plugin")

    fun buildConfig(
        node: ParsedNode,
        options: SingboxConfigOptions = SingboxConfigOptions()
    ): String {
        return buildConfig(listOf(node), node, options)
    }

    /**
     * Loopback port of the in-app obfs2/obfs3 pluggable-transport relay. The
     * relay speaks SOCKS5 to the core and obfs to the bridge, so no external
     * obfsproxy binary has to be shipped.
     */
    const val OBFS_LOCAL_PORT = 10871

    fun buildConfig(
        nodes: List<ParsedNode>,
        selectedNode: ParsedNode?,
        options: SingboxConfigOptions = SingboxConfigOptions()
    ): String {
        val root = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()

        // 1. Log
        root["log"] = mapOf(
            "level" to (options.logLevel.takeIf { it in LOG_LEVELS } ?: "info"),
            "timestamp" to true
        )

        // 2. Inbounds (TUN + optional local SOCKS/HTTP). The shipped app always
        // passes tunMode = false: VpnService owns the tun device and hev
        // tun2socks feeds the local SOCKS inbound. The tun branch (and tunMtu)
        // only exists for tests and desktop-shaped configs.
        val inbounds = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()
        if (options.tunMode) {
            inbounds.add(
                mapOf(
                    "type" to "tun",
                    "tag" to "tun-in",
                    "interface_name" to "singbox_tun",
                    "address" to listOf(options.tunAddressIPv4, options.tunAddressIPv6),
                    "mtu" to options.tunMtu,
                    "auto_route" to true,
                    "strict_route" to options.tunStrictRoute,
                    "stack" to options.tunStack
                )
            )
        }

        // "Allow connections from LAN" only widens the bind address; tun2socks
        // keeps reaching the same inbound over loopback either way.
        val listenAddress = if (options.allowLanConnections) "0.0.0.0" else "127.0.0.1"

        // Socks5 authorization. Only complete credentials switch it on, so an
        // empty preference can never publish an inbound nothing can reach.
        val socksUsername = options.socksUsername
        val socksPassword = options.socksPassword
        if (options.socksAuthEnabled) {
            require(listOf(socksUsername, socksPassword).all { value ->
                value.length in 1..255 && value.none { it.code < 32 || it.code == 127 } &&
                    Charsets.UTF_8.newEncoder().canEncode(value) && value.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8).size <= 255
            }) { "SOCKS5 credentials must each contain 1–255 UTF-8 bytes and no control characters" }
        }
        val socksAuthActive = options.socksAuthEnabled

        if (options.localSocksPort > 0) {
            val socksIn = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
                "type" to "socks",
                "tag" to "socks-in",
                "listen" to listenAddress,
                "listen_port" to options.localSocksPort,
                "udp_fragment" to true
            )
            if (socksAuthActive) {
                socksIn["users"] = listOf(
                    mapOf("username" to socksUsername, "password" to socksPassword)
                )
            }
            // How long an idle UDP association is kept. The core's own default
            // (5m) is left untouched while the user has not chosen a value.
            if (options.inboundUdpTimeoutSeconds > 0) {
                socksIn["udp_timeout"] = "${options.inboundUdpTimeoutSeconds.coerceIn(1, 3600)}s"
            }
            inbounds.add(socksIn)
        }

        // The HTTP inbound has no credentials of its own here, so publishing it
        // next to an authenticated SOCKS inbound would reopen the same hole.
        if (!socksAuthActive &&
            options.localHttpPort > 0 && options.localHttpPort != options.localSocksPort
        ) {
            inbounds.add(
                mapOf(
                    "type" to "http",
                    "tag" to "http-in",
                    "listen" to listenAddress,
                    "listen_port" to options.localHttpPort
                )
            )
        }

        root["inbounds"] = inbounds

        // 3. Outbounds
        val outbounds = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()
        val dependencyOutbounds = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()
        val dependencyEndpoints = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()
        val activeNode = selectedNode ?: nodes.firstOrNull()
            ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("No server selected")
        val activeType = activeNode.outbound["type"]?.toString()?.lowercase()
        val isAuto = activeNode.scheme.equals("auto", true) ||
            activeType in setOf("urltest", "selector", "fallback", "failover", "bond")
        val activePoolNodes = if (isAuto) {
            LinkParser.autoMembers(activeNode.outbound)
                .ifEmpty { nodes.filterNot { it.scheme.equals("auto", true) } }
        } else {
            emptyList()
        }
        // Native composite configs retain their original dependency tags. Avoid
        // assigning the same proxy-N tag to an AUTO member: duplicate tags are
        // ambiguous in the core and can redirect a detour to the wrong outbound.
        val nativeDependencyTags = activePoolNodes.flatMap { poolNode ->
            val closure = mapValue(poolNode.outbound["_singbox_dependencies"])
            listOf("outbounds", "endpoints").flatMap { key ->
                (closure?.get(key) as? List<*>)
                    ?.mapNotNull(::mapValue)
                    ?.mapNotNull { it["tag"]?.toString()?.trim()?.takeIf(String::isNotEmpty) }
                    .orEmpty()
            }
        }.toSet()

        run {
            if (isAuto) {
                // Auto Virtual Node: an imported AUTO group carries its own pool of
                // servers; a manually created one falls back to every other server.
                val poolTags = mutableListOf<String>()
                val failures = mutableListOf<String>()

                for (poolNode in activePoolNodes) {
                    var tagIndex = poolTags.size
                    var tag = "proxy-$tagIndex"
                    while (tag in nativeDependencyTags || tag in poolTags) {
                        tag = "proxy-${++tagIndex}"
                    }
                    // One broken server must not invalidate the whole auto pool.
                    val candidateDependencyOutbounds = dependencyOutbounds.toMutableList()
                    val candidateDependencyEndpoints = dependencyEndpoints.toMutableList()
                    val ob = try {
                        buildOutboundMap(poolNode, tag, options).also {
                            // Build dependencies transactionally. A malformed nested
                            // Shadowsocks hop must drop only this AUTO member, without
                            // leaving half of its dependency chain in the document.
                            collectSingboxDependencies(
                                poolNode,
                                candidateDependencyOutbounds,
                                candidateDependencyEndpoints,
                                options
                            )
                        }
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        failures += "${poolNode.name.ifBlank { poolNode.server }}: ${e.message ?: "invalid config"}"
                        null
                    }
                    if (ob == null || ob["type"] == null) continue
                    dependencyOutbounds.clear()
                    dependencyOutbounds.addAll(candidateDependencyOutbounds)
                    dependencyEndpoints.clear()
                    dependencyEndpoints.addAll(candidateDependencyEndpoints)
                    outbounds.add(ob)
                    poolTags.add(tag)
                }

                if (poolTags.isEmpty()) {
                    val details = failures.take(3).joinToString("; ")
                    throw IllegalArgumentException(
                        "AUTO `${activeNode.name}` has no usable servers" +
                            if (details.isBlank()) "" else ": $details"
                    )
                }
                val autoOutbound = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
                    "type" to "urltest",
                    "tag" to "proxy",
                    "outbounds" to poolTags,
                    "url" to options.urlTestUrl,
                    "interval" to "${options.urlTestIntervalMinutes.coerceIn(1, 1440)}m",
                    "tolerance" to options.urlTestToleranceMs.coerceIn(0, 5000),
                    "interrupt_exist_connections" to options.urlTestInterruptExistConnections
                )
                // How long an unused member keeps being probed. On a 300-member
                // pool the periodic sweep is the dominant cost, so a short idle
                // timeout is the knob that actually helps.
                if (options.urlTestIdleTimeoutMinutes > 0) {
                    autoOutbound["idle_timeout"] = "${options.urlTestIdleTimeoutMinutes.coerceIn(1, 1440)}m"
                }
                outbounds.addAll(0, dependencyOutbounds)
                outbounds.add(0, autoOutbound)
            } else {
                // Single node
                val ob = buildOutboundMap(activeNode, "proxy", options)
                collectSingboxDependencies(
                    activeNode,
                    dependencyOutbounds,
                    dependencyEndpoints,
                    options
                )
                outbounds.addAll(dependencyOutbounds)
                outbounds.add(ob)
            }
        }

        // Auxiliary Outbounds. Note: the legacy special "block" outbound was
        // removed in sing-box 1.13; blocking is done via `action: reject` rules.
        outbounds.add(mapOf("type" to "direct", "tag" to "direct"))

        // "Use proxy" for OpenVPN: the parser stores the selection under
        // `lumen_proxy`, which the core does not know. Turn it into a real detour
        // outbound and strip the marker key. obfs2/obfs3 are terminated by the
        // in-app relay, so their detour points at loopback.
        val detourOutbounds = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any?>>()
        outbounds.forEachIndexed { index, ob ->
            val proxy = ob["lumen_proxy"] as? Map<*, *> ?: return@forEachIndexed
            val cleaned = ob.toMutableMap()
            cleaned.remove("lumen_proxy")
            val detourTag = "ovpn-proxy-$index"
            cleaned["detour"] = detourTag
            outbounds[index] = cleaned
            val type = proxy["type"]?.toString().orEmpty()
            val detour = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>("tag" to detourTag)
            if (type == "http" || type == "socks") {
                detour["type"] = type
                detour["server"] = proxy["server"]?.toString().orEmpty()
                detour["server_port"] = (proxy["server_port"] as? Number)?.toInt()
                    ?: proxy["server_port"]?.toString()?.toIntOrNull() ?: 0
                proxy["username"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let { detour["username"] = it }
                proxy["password"]?.toString()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let { detour["password"] = it }
            } else {
                detour["type"] = "socks"
                detour["server"] = "127.0.0.1"
                detour["server_port"] =
                    options.obfsLocalPort.takeIf { it in 1..65535 } ?: OBFS_LOCAL_PORT
            }
            detourOutbounds.add(detour)
        }
        outbounds.addAll(detourOutbounds)

        // Final masque sweep. buildOutboundMap only sanitizes the outbounds it
        // builds itself, so masque nodes that arrive from the database, from an
        // AUTO pool member, or from a raw imported config could still carry
        // `profile.server` and abort the whole config with
        // `outbounds[N].profile.server: json: unknown field "server"`.
        for (index in outbounds.indices) {
            if (outbounds[index]["type"]?.toString()?.lowercase() != "masque") continue
            val sanitized = outbounds[index].toMutableMap()
            sanitizeMasqueOutbound(sanitized)
            outbounds[index] = sanitized
        }

        root["outbounds"] = outbounds
        if (dependencyEndpoints.isNotEmpty()) {
            root["endpoints"] = dependencyEndpoints
        }

        // 4. User Routing Rules (Domains, Geosite, IP CIDRs). Classified before
        // the DNS section because the direct-DNS rule is built from the result:
        // a `proxy:`/`block:` entry or a geosite:/geoip: code must never end up
        // in the direct resolver rule.
        data class DomainPattern(val field: String, val value: String)
        data class UserRouteRule(
            val kind: String,
            val field: String,
            val value: String,
            val action: String
        )
        val directDomains = mutableListOf<DomainPattern>()
        val orderedUserRules = mutableListOf<UserRouteRule>()

        fun processRuleItem(item: String) {
            val trimmed = item.trim()
            if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return
            val action = when {
                trimmed.startsWith("proxy:", true) -> "proxy"
                trimmed.startsWith("block:", true) || trimmed.startsWith("reject:", true) -> "block"
                trimmed.startsWith("direct:", true) -> "direct"
                else -> "direct"
            }
            // Only strip a leading action prefix, so bare "geosite:ru" keeps its kind.
            val rawPattern = listOf("proxy:", "block:", "reject:", "direct:")
                .firstOrNull { trimmed.startsWith(it, true) }
                ?.let { trimmed.substring(it.length).trim() }
                ?.ifEmpty { trimmed }
                ?: trimmed

            val isGeosite = rawPattern.startsWith("geosite:", true)
            val isGeoip = rawPattern.startsWith("geoip:", true)
            val isIp = !isGeosite && !isGeoip &&
